                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Cautions and limits

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Stop codes suggest investigation areas; they are not certain diagnoses. Kernel-Power event 41 alone does not identify the cause.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Back up data before repair. CHKDSK cannot physically repair a failing disk. A /r scan can take hours and may need a restart.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • The words bash and Copy in the earlier pasted explanation were formatting labels. SFC and CHKDSK are Windows commands.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Back to menu

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Windows keys and official activation

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Licensing and activation · practical guidance

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Key points

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Keep real product keys private.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Steps and practical use

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Use the console licensing commands in Licensing and activation.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Use official activation or your organization's licensed activation method.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Cautions and important differences

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Firmware keys can be absent; keys must match the licensed edition.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Never include recovered keys in shared logs.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Do not run unreviewed remote activation scripts.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Do not change execution policy or Script Host merely to bypass an organization policy.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Back to menu

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      PowerShell source corrections

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      PowerShell and scripting · practical guidance

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Key points

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Syntax diagrams such as <string> and [-Name] are documentation, not paste-ready commands.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Get-WmiObject/Get-EventLog are Windows PowerShell legacy commands; prefer CIM/Get-WinEvent in PowerShell 7.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Help -ShowWindow is graphical.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Date formats, operators and sample functions are learning material, not repairs.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Steps and practical use

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Use documentation that matches your installed PowerShell version.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Cautions and important differences

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Use "$($city.Length)" for property expansion inside strings. "$city.length" is not equivalent in PowerShell 5.1.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • The expression $msg = "Hello World") has an unmatched closing parenthesis.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Install-WUUpdates is not a built-in cmdlet; identify its supplying module before considering it.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Back to menu

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Keyboard layouts and Windows-key shortcuts

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Languages and keyboards · practical guidance

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Key points

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • British en-GB: 00000809.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • US en-US: 00000409.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Danish da-DK: 00000406.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • German Germany de-DE: 00000407.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • These are standard layouts, not variants such as US International or Swiss German.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • German uses QWERTZ, swaps Y/Z, and includes Ä Ö Ü ß.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Steps and practical use

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Win+Space switches installed layouts; Win+I opens Settings, then Time & language > Language & region > Language options > Keyboards.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Win+X opens administrative shortcuts; Win+R opens Run.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Get-WinUserLanguageList inspects the current user; preserve existing language lists when adding a layout.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Danish, US and British layouts use QWERTY.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Cautions and important differences

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Physical key labels do not change with the selected layout.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Back to menu

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      God Mode and Control Panel

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Windows configuration · practical guidance

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Key points

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • God Mode is a graphical index of existing Control Panel tasks, not an elevated repair mode or a complete list of every Windows setting.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Some tasks vary by Windows build and edition.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • GUI tools are listed in the manual; the terminal menu does not launch them. sysdm.cpl opens System Properties and computer-name/domain settings, not Add Hardware. hdwwiz.exe opens the Add Hardware wizard; hdwwiz.cpl usually opens Device Manager.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Several original applets belong to older Windows versions and are marked legacy.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Back to menu

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Group Policy, backups and rollback

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Accounts and security · practical guidance

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Key points

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Registry and firewall toolkit changes save the previous selected values, with restore actions in the same menu.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Steps and practical use

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Use gpresult to inspect effective policy before changing settings.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Feature changes use NoRestart; reboot manually when required.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Account creation and memberships require explicit technician choices; retain a working admin account.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Cautions and important differences

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Managed domain/MDM policy can override local changes. gpupdate refreshes policy; it does not remove restrictive settings.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • A backup of one registry value or firewall profile is not a complete configuration backup.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Back to menu

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Windows Server and domain scope

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Server administration · practical guidance

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Key points

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Server Core may lack graphical tools.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • No production domain query was executed during this rebuild.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Steps and practical use

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Server/AD/DNS/DHCP tools require the installed role or RSAT modules plus authorized connectivity.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Cautions and important differences

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Windows Sandbox does not reproduce a domain or every Windows Server role.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Placeholders such as yourdomain.local need the actual authorized lab target.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Back to menu

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      PowerShell setup: scripts are disabled

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      PowerShell and scripting · practical guidance

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Meaning of the error: the setup decoded the toolkit, but Windows blocked execution of the saved PowerShell script. The support menu has not started.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Check the policy first

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Open Windows PowerShell and run Get-ExecutionPolicy -List. This only reads policy settings.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Read MachinePolicy and UserPolicy first. If either is configured, follow the organization-approved process; local policy changes cannot override these scopes.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • For a company workstation, ask the IT administrator for an approved or signed toolkit and the permitted execution workflow.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      If you own the PC and are authorized to change policy

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      1. Review the toolkit code and confirm that its source is trusted.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      2. When no organization policy applies, RemoteSigned at CurrentUser can allow local scripts while still requiring signatures for scripts marked as downloaded.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      3. The command Set-ExecutionPolicy RemoteSigned -Scope CurrentUser is a persistent setting change, not a read-only fix. Record the previous CurrentUser setting and use only when your policy permits it.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      4. Rerun the menu setup after an approved policy change. To undo a change, restore the recorded previous setting; do not assume every PC previously used Restricted.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Important limits

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Running as Administrator does not automatically override execution policy.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • Organization controls, application allowlisting and script signing requirements may still block execution.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      • There is no execution-policy bypass in either menu setup. CMD users must still enter PowerShell before pasting a setup block.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Back to menu

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ASCII keyboard diagrams and key maps

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Standard German, Danish, US and British layouts. Named labels keep the table ASCII.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      WINDOWS KEYBOARD MAPS - ASCII KEY MAPS
Labels: ae/AE = U+00E6/U+00C6; oe/OE = U+00F8/U+00D8;
a-ring/A-ring = U+00E5/U+00C5; a-/o-/u-umlaut = U+00E4/00F6/00FC;
sharp-s = U+00DF; GBP = U+00A3; EUR = U+20AC; section = U+00A7.
Rows show UNshifted keys; letter capitals below label their positions.
ISO means the extra key between Left Shift and the first letter.

US (00000409) - QWERTY
+----------------------------------------------------------------+
| ` | 1 2 3 4 5 6 7 8 9 0 | - = | Backspace                    |
| Tab | Q W E R T Y U I O P | [ ] | \                           |
| Caps | A S D F G H J K L | ; ' | Enter                       |
| Shift | Z X C V B N M | , . / | Shift                        |
+----------------------------------------------------------------+
BRITISH / UK (00000809) - QWERTY
+----------------------------------------------------------------+
| ` | 1 2 3 4 5 6 7 8 9 0 | - = | Backspace                    |
| Tab | Q W E R T Y U I O P | [ ] | Enter                       |
| Caps | A S D F G H J K L | ; ' # | Enter                      |
| Shift | \ | Z X C V B N M | , . / | Shift                    |
+----------------------------------------------------------------+
DANISH (00000406) - QWERTY
+----------------------------------------------------------------+
| half | 1 2 3 4 5 6 7 8 9 0 | + acute | Backspace             |
| Tab | Q W E R T Y U I O P | a-ring diaeresis | Enter          |
| Caps | A S D F G H J K L | ae oe apostrophe | Enter           |
| Shift | < | Z X C V B N M | , . - | Shift                    |
+----------------------------------------------------------------+
GERMAN / GERMANY (00000407) - QWERTZ
+----------------------------------------------------------------+
| circumflex | 1 2 3 4 5 6 7 8 9 0 | sharp-s acute | Backspace  |
| Tab | Q W E R T Z U I O P | u-umlaut + | Enter                |
| Caps | A S D F G H J K L | o-umlaut a-umlaut # | Enter        |
| Shift | < | Y X C V B N M | , . - | Shift                    |
+----------------------------------------------------------------+

SHIFTED NUMBER ROW (physical 1 through 0)
+---------+---+---+---------+----------+---+---+---+---+---+---+
| Layout  | 1 | 2 | 3       | 4        | 5 | 6 | 7 | 8 | 9 | 0 |
+---------+---+---+---------+----------+---+---+---+---+---+---+
| US      | ! | @ | #       | $        | % | ^ | & | * | ( | ) |
| British | ! | " | GBP     | $        | % | ^ | & | * | ( | ) |
| Danish  | ! | " | #       | currency | % | & | / | ( | ) | = |
| German  | ! | " | section | $        | % | & | / | ( | ) | = |
+---------+---+---+---------+----------+---+---+---+---+---+---+
Danish currency sign is U+00A4, not dollar.

SCRIPT SYMBOLS - ACTIVE LAYOUT KEY COMBINATIONS
+--------+---------+-------------+-------------+---------------+
| Symbol | US      | British     | Danish      | German        |
+--------+---------+-------------+-------------+---------------+
| @      | Shift+2 | Shift+'     | AltGr+2     | AltGr+Q       |
| "      | Shift+' | Shift+2     | Shift+2     | Shift+2       |
| '      | ' key   | ' key       | ' key       | Shift+#       |
| #      | Shift+3 | # key       | Shift+3     | # key         |
| $      | Shift+4 | Shift+4     | AltGr+4     | Shift+4       |
| \      | \ key   | ISO \ key   | AltGr+acute | AltGr+sharp-s |
| |      | Shift+\ | Shift+ISO \ | AltGr+ISO < | AltGr+ISO <   |
| [      | [ key   | [ key       | AltGr+8     | AltGr+8       |
| ]      | ] key   | ] key       | AltGr+9     | AltGr+9       |
| {      | Shift+[ | Shift+[     | AltGr+7     | AltGr+7       |
| }      | Shift+] | Shift+]     | AltGr+0     | AltGr+0       |
| /      | / key   | / key       | Shift+7     | Shift+7       |
| :      | Shift+; | Shift+;     | Shift+.     | Shift+.       |
| ;      | ; key   | ; key       | Shift+,     | Shift+,       |
| _      | Shift+- | Shift+-     | Shift+-     | Shift+-       |
| <      | Shift+, | Shift+,     | ISO < key   | ISO < key     |
| >      | Shift+. | Shift+.     | Shift+ISO < | Shift+ISO <   |
+--------+---------+-------------+-------------+---------------+

AltGr is right Alt. Physical key legends may not match the active layout.
German swaps Y/Z. AltGr+E gives EUR on standard German and Danish layouts.
Dead accents wait for a following character; use Space for a literal accent.
These maps do not cover US International, UK Extended or Swiss German.
Win+Space switches installed layouts. Check Settings > Time & language
and the on-screen keyboard when keys do not produce expected characters.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Validation and practical limits

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      The recovered PowerShell toolkit matches SHA-256 38fef8d92dfa14a049608799b04a84e59f09c8a4e84020631fa2f8aa559daf14. This release checks command deduplication, menu links, embedded payload hashes, script syntax and browser behavior. Detailed results are included in the release package.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Real Windows effects, CMD execution, feature transitions and domain/server operations have not been tested here: the build environment is Linux. Earlier mock test results are not claimed as current Windows validation. Run changes in an isolated Windows lab before company deployment. Completion of a command does not prove a symptom is fixed.
